571|export const SceneCard = ({ scene, entities, shotCount = 0, onClick, onGene
rateShots, onSupplementShots, onDelete, selected = false, onToggleSelect, uiLan

578|    useEffect(() => {
579|        // Parse logic
580|        const sourceText = scene.environment_name || scene.location || '';
581|        let anchors = [];
582|        const bracketMatches = sourceText.match(/\[(.*?)\]/g);
583|        if (bracketMatches && bracketMatches.length > 0) {
584|            anchors = bracketMatches.map(m => m.replace(/[\[\]\*]/g, '').tr
im());
585|        } else {
586|            anchors = sourceText.split(/[,锛宂/).map(s => s.replace(/[\*]/g, 
'').trim()).filter(Boolean);
587|        }
588|
589|        const validUrls = [];
590|        // Updated cleaner: Removes whitespace to handle "涓昏瑙? vs "涓昏瑙?" 
mismatch
591|        const cleanForMatch = (str) => (str || '').replace(/[锛圽(\)锛塡s]/g, '
').toLowerCase();
592|
593|        // 棰勫鐞嗘墍鏈夊疄浣擄紝閬垮厤鍦ㄥ弻灞傚惊鐜腑閲嶅鎵ц鏄傝吹鐨勬鍒欏拰瀛楃涓叉搷浣?
594|        const parsedEntities = (entities || []).map(e => {
595|            const cn = cleanForMatch(e.name);

597|            if (!en && e.description) {

]+)/i);

im().toLowerCase();

nClean === targetName);

tName.includes(m.enClean)))

869|                                                {prop.trim()}

871|                                        ))}

875|                            </>

enter gap-1 disabled:opacity-60"
890|                            title={t('AI 鐢熸垚闀滃ご鍒楄〃', 'AI Generate Shot List

1358|    const filteredScenes = useMemo(() => {

e, sensitivity: 'base' });

'), undefined, { numeric: true, sensitivity: 'base' });
1368|            });

1376|            if (tb !== ta) return tb - ta;
1377|            const ka = getSceneOrderKey(a);
1378|            const kb = getSceneOrderKey(b);
1379|            return ka.localeCompare(kb, undefined, { numeric: true, sensit
ivity: 'base' });

1526|    const splitCombinedCnPrompt = (raw) => {
1527|        const textValue = String(raw || '').trim();
1528|        if (!textValue) {
1529|            return {
1530|                start_frame_cn: '',
1531|                video_prompt_cn: '',
1532|                keyframes_cn: '',
1533|                end_frame_cn: '',
1534|            };
1535|        }
1536|
1537|        const lines = textValue
1538|            .split(/\n|<br\s*\/?>/i)
1539|            .map((line) => String(line || '').trim())
1540|            .filter(Boolean);
1541|
1542|        let start = '';
1543|        let video = '';
1544|        let keyframes = '';
1545|        let end = '';
1546|
1547|        lines.forEach((line) => {
1548|            const lower = line.toLowerCase();
1549|            if (/^(start\s*frame\s*(cn)?\s*:|start\s*:|璧峰甯s*[:锛歖)/.test(
lower) || /^璧峰甯s*[:锛歖/.test(line)) {
1550|                start = line.replace(/^(start\s*frame\s*(cn)?\s*:|start\s*
:|璧峰甯s*[:锛歖)/i, '').trim();
1551|                return;
1552|            }
1553|            if (/^(video\s*(cn)?\s*:|瑙嗛鎻愮ず璇峔s*[:锛歖|瑙嗛\s*[:锛歖)/.test(lowe
r) || /^瑙嗛(鎻愮ず璇??\s*[:锛歖/.test(line)) {
1554|                video = line.replace(/^(video\s*(cn)?\s*:|瑙嗛鎻愮ず璇峔s*[:锛歖|瑙
嗛\s*[:锛歖)/i, '').trim();
1555|                return;
1556|            }
1557|            if (/^(key\s*frames?\s*(cn)?\s*:|鍏抽敭甯s*[:锛歖)/.test(lower) || 
/^鍏抽敭甯s*[:锛歖/.test(line)) {
1558|                keyframes = line.replace(/^(key\s*frames?\s*(cn)?\s*:|鍏抽敭甯
s*[:锛歖)/i, '').trim();
1559|                return;
1560|            }
1561|            if (/^(end\s*frame\s*(cn)?\s*:|end\s*:|鏀跺熬甯s*[:锛歖|缁撴潫甯s*[:锛歖
)/.test(lower) || /^(鏀跺熬甯缁撴潫甯?\s*[:锛歖/.test(line)) {
1562|                end = line.replace(/^(end\s*frame\s*(cn)?\s*:|end\s*:|鏀跺熬甯
s*[:锛歖|缁撴潫甯s*[:锛歖)/i, '').trim();
1563|            }
1564|        });
1565|
1566|        if (!start && !video && !keyframes && !end) {
1567|            return {
1568|                start_frame_cn: textValue,
1569|                video_prompt_cn: textValue,
1570|                keyframes_cn: textValue,
1571|                end_frame_cn: textValue,
1572|            };
1573|        }
1574|
1575|        if (!end && start) end = start;
1576|
1577|        return {
1578|            start_frame_cn: start,
1579|            video_prompt_cn: video,
1580|            keyframes_cn: keyframes,
1581|            end_frame_cn: end,
1582|        };
1583|    };

2066|    const parseScenesFromText = useCallback((text) => {
2067|        if (!text) return [];
2068|        const lines = text.split('\n').filter(l => l.trim().includes('|'))
;
2069|        const headerIdx = lines.findIndex(l =>
2070|            (l.includes("Scene No") || l.includes("鍦烘搴忓彿") || l.includes(
"Scene ID") || l.includes("鍦烘") || l.includes("Title"))
2071|        );
2072|
2073|        if (headerIdx === -1) return [];
2074|
2075|        const headerLine = lines[headerIdx];
2076|        let headers = headerLine.split('|').map(c => c.trim());
2077|        if (headers.length > 0 && headers[0] === "") headers.shift();
2078|        if (headers.length > 0 && headers[headers.length - 1] === "") head
ers.pop();
2079|
2080|        const normalizeHeader = (h) => h.toLowerCase().replace(/[\.\s]/g, 
'');
2081|        const headerMap = {};
2082|        headers.forEach((h, idx) => {
2083|            const n = normalizeHeader(h);
2084|            if (n.includes("episodeid") || n.includes("闆唅d")) headerMap['e
pisode_id'] = idx;
2085|            else if ((n.includes("sceneid") && !n.includes("sceneno")) || 
n.includes("鍦烘櫙id")) headerMap['scene_id'] = idx;
2086|            if (n.includes("sceneno") || n.includes("鍦烘")) headerMap['sce
ne_no'] = idx;
2087|            else if (n.includes("scenename") || n.includes("title")) heade
rMap['scene_name'] = idx;
2088|            else if (n.includes("equivalentduration")) headerMap['equivale
nt_duration'] = idx;
2089|            else if (n.includes("coresceneinfo") || n.includes("coregoal")
) headerMap['core_scene_info'] = idx;
2090|            else if (n.includes("originalscripttext") || n.includes("descr
iption")) headerMap['original_script_text'] = idx;
2091|            else if (n.includes("environmentname") || n.includes("environm
ent")) headerMap['environment_name'] = idx;
2092|            else if (n.includes("environmentrelation")) headerMap['environ
ment_relation'] = idx;
2093|            else if (n.includes("entrystate")) headerMap['entry_state'] = 
idx;
2094|            else if (n.includes("exitstate")) headerMap['exit_state'] = id
x;
2095|            else if (n.includes("linkedcharacters")) headerMap['linked_cha
racters'] = idx;
2096|            else if (n.includes("keyprops")) headerMap['key_props'] = idx;
2097|        });
2098|
2099|        const rows = [];
2100|        let inShotTable = false;
2101|
2102|        for (let i = headerIdx + 1; i < lines.length; i++) {
2103|            const line = lines[i];
2104|            if (line.includes("Shot ID") || line.includes("闀滃ごID")) {
2105|                inShotTable = true;
2106|                continue;
2107|            }
2108|            if (line.includes("Scene No") || line.includes("鍦烘搴忓彿")) {
2109|                inShotTable = false;
2110|                continue;
2111|            }
2112|            if (inShotTable) continue;
2113|            if (line.includes('---')) continue;
2114|
2115|            let cols = line.split('|').map(c => c.trim());
2116|            if (cols.length > 0 && cols[0] === "") cols.shift();
2117|            if (cols.length > 0 && cols[cols.length - 1] === "") cols.pop(
);
2118|
2119|            if (cols.length >= 2) {
2120|                const cleanCol = (txt) => txt ? txt.replace(/<br\s*\/?>/gi
, '\n').replace(/\\\|/g, '|') : '';
2121|                const getVal = (key, fallbackIdx) => {
2122|                    const idx = headerMap[key] !== undefined ? headerMap[k
ey] : fallbackIdx;
2123|                    return cols[idx] ? cleanCol(cols[idx]) : '';
2124|                };
2125|
2126|                const isNewFormat = cols.length >= 13 || headerMap['episod
e_id'] !== undefined || headerMap['scene_id'] !== undefined;
2127|                const fallback = isNewFormat
2128|                    ? {
2129|                        scene_no: 2,
2130|                        scene_name: 3,
2131|                        equivalent_duration: 4,
2132|                        core_scene_info: 5,
2133|                        original_script_text: 6,
2134|                        environment_name: 7,
2135|                        linked_characters: 11,
2136|                        key_props: 12,
2137|                    }
2138|                    : {
2139|                        scene_no: 0,
2140|                        scene_name: 1,
2141|                        equivalent_duration: 2,
2142|                        core_scene_info: 3,
2143|                        original_script_text: 4,
2144|                        environment_name: 5,
2145|                        linked_characters: 6,
2146|                        key_props: 7,
2147|                    };
2148|
2149|                rows.push({
2150|                    scene_no: getVal('scene_no', fallback.scene_no),
2151|                    scene_name: getVal('scene_name', fallback.scene_name),
2152|                    equivalent_duration: getVal('equivalent_duration', fal
lback.equivalent_duration),
2153|                    core_scene_info: getVal('core_scene_info', fallback.co
re_scene_info),
2154|                    original_script_text: normalizeOriginalScriptText(getV
al('original_script_text', fallback.original_script_text)),
2155|                    environment_name: getVal('environment_name', fallback.
environment_name),
2156|                    linked_characters: getVal('linked_characters', fallbac
k.linked_characters),
2157|                    key_props: getVal('key_props', fallback.key_props)
2158|                });
2159|            }
2160|        }
2161|        return rows;
2162|    }, []);

2330|    const buildSceneContentMarkdown = (sceneRows = []) => {
2331|        if (!activeEpisode) return '';
2332|        const contextInfo = `Project: ${project?.title || 'Unknown'} | Epi
sode: ${activeEpisode?.title || 'Unknown'}\n`;
2333|        const header = `| Episode ID | Scene ID | Scene No. | Scene Name |
 Equivalent Duration | Core Scene Info | Original Script Text | Environment Nam
e | Environment Relation | Entry State | Exit State | Linked Characters | Key P
rops |\n|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|`;
2334|        const clean = (txt) => {
2335|            let normalized = '';
2336|            if (txt === null || txt === undefined) {
2337|                normalized = '';
2338|            } else if (typeof txt === 'string') {
2339|                normalized = txt;
2340|            } else if (typeof txt === 'number' || typeof txt === 'boolean'
) {
2341|                normalized = String(txt);
2342|            } else {
2343|                try {
2344|                    normalized = JSON.stringify(txt);
2345|                } catch {
2346|                    normalized = String(txt);
2347|                }
2348|            }
2349|            return normalized.replace(/\n/g, '<br>').replace(/\|/g, '\\|')
;
2350|        };
2351|        const content = (sceneRows || []).map((s) => (
2352|            `| ${clean(activeEpisode?.id)} | ${clean(s.id)} | ${clean(s.sc
ene_no)} | ${clean(s.scene_name)} | ${clean(s.equivalent_duration)} | ${clean(s
.core_scene_info)} | ${clean(s.original_script_text)} | ${clean(s.environment_n
ame)} | ${clean(s.environment_relation || '')} | ${clean(s.entry_state || '')} 
| ${clean(s.exit_state || '')} | ${clean(s.linked_characters)} | ${clean(s.key_
props)} |`
2353|        )).join('\n');
2354|        return `${contextInfo}${header}\n${content}`;
2355|    };

2590|    const flushSceneAutoSave = useCallback(async (sceneCandidate) => {
2591|        if (!activeEpisode?.id || !sceneCandidate?.id) return;
2592|
2593|        const payload = buildSceneSavePayload(sceneCandidate);
2594|        const snapshot = JSON.stringify(payload);
2595|        const lastSnapshot = sceneAutoSavedSnapshotRef.current;
2596|        if (lastSnapshot.sceneId === sceneCandidate.id && lastSnapshot.sna
pshot === snapshot) return;

2598|        if (sceneAutoSaveInFlightRef.current) {
2599|            sceneAutoSaveQueuedRef.current = sceneCandidate;
2600|            return;
2601|        }
2602|
2603|        sceneAutoSaveInFlightRef.current = true;
2604|        try {
2605|            await updateScene(sceneCandidate.id, payload);
2606|            const nextScenes = (scenes || []).map((sceneRow) => (
2607|                sceneRow.id === sceneCandidate.id ? { ...sceneRow, ...payl
oad } : sceneRow

...prev, ...payload } : prev));
2611|            await updateEpisode(activeEpisode.id, { scene_content: buildSc
eneContentMarkdown(nextScenes) });
2612|            sceneAutoSavedSnapshotRef.current = { sceneId: sceneCandidate.
id, snapshot };
2613|        } catch (e) {
2614|            onLog?.(`Scene auto-save failed - ${e?.message || 'Unknown err
or'}`, 'error');
2615|        } finally {
2616|            sceneAutoSaveInFlightRef.current = false;
2617|            const queued = sceneAutoSaveQueuedRef.current;
2618|            sceneAutoSaveQueuedRef.current = null;
2619|            if (queued?.id) {
2620|                void flushSceneAutoSave(queued);
2621|            }
2622|        }

n, onLog, scenes]);

2625|    const scheduleSceneAutoSave = useCallback((sceneCandidate) => {
2626|        if (!activeEpisode?.id || !sceneCandidate?.id) return;
2627|        sceneAutoSaveDraftRef.current = sceneCandidate;

2629|            clearTimeout(sceneAutoSaveTimerRef.current);
2630|            sceneAutoSaveTimerRef.current = null;
2631|        }

2633|            const latest = sceneAutoSaveDraftRef.current;
2634|            if (latest?.id) {
2635|                void flushSceneAutoSave(latest);

2973|    const getSceneImage = (scene) => {
2974|        // Use environment_name as requested, cleaning markdown ** and [] 
      
2975|        const sourceText = scene.environment_name || scene.location || '';
      
2976|        const rawLoc = sourceText.replace(/[\[\]\*]/g, '').trim().toLowerC
ase();
2977|
2978|        if (!rawLoc) return null;
2979|
2980|        const cleanForMatch = (str) => (str || '').replace(/[锛圽(\)锛塢/g, ''
).trim().toLowerCase();
2981|        const targetName = cleanForMatch(rawLoc);
2982|
2983|        // Try exact match first
2984|        let match = parsedEntitiesForImage.find(m => m.cn === targetName |
| m.enClean === targetName);
2985|
2986|        // Try fuzzy match if exact fails
2987|        if (!match) {
2988|             match = parsedEntitiesForImage.find(m => 
2989|                 (m.cn && (m.cn.includes(targetName) || targetName.include
s(m.cn))) ||
2990|                 (m.enClean && (m.enClean.includes(targetName) || targetNa
me.includes(m.enClean)))
2991|             );
2992|        }
2993|
2994|        return match ? match.raw.image_url : null;

=> {

3839|        if (targets.length === 0) return;

| e?.message || 'delete failed'}`);

has(getSceneSelectionKey(scene)));

.has(key)));

ldSceneContentMarkdown(remaining) });
